## q01 · 玩家如何评价 Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 的剧情和角色？

What do players think of the story and characters in Clair Obscur: Expedition 33?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
The supplied reviews praise Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 for an emotionally gripping narrative, strong writing and characters, and memorable world-building. A review with 3 helpful votes finds the story very well written but says it slows in the middle before picking up again.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
这些评论称赞 Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 的叙事富有感染力，写作和角色表现出色，世界塑造令人印象深刻。一条获 3 次有用投票的评论认为剧情写得很好，但中段略显停滞，之后又重新精彩起来。

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：这些评论称赞 Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 的叙事富有感染力，写作和角色表现出色，世界塑造令人印象深刻。

## q02 · Hollow Knight 的配乐怎么样？

How is the soundtrack in Hollow Knight?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
Reviewers praise Hollow Knight's soundtrack as phenomenal and hauntingly beautiful. One review highlights Christopher Larkin's score as part of the atmosphere of Hallownest's ruins and forgotten kingdom.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
评论者称赞 Hollow Knight 的配乐十分出色，具有令人难忘的幽美氛围。一条评论特别提到 Christopher Larkin 的配乐，认为它融入了圣巢遗迹与失落王国的氛围。

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：评论者称赞 Hollow Knight 的配乐十分出色，具有令人难忘的幽美氛围。
  - English: Reviewers praise Hollow Knight's soundtrack as phenomenal and hauntingly beautiful.
  - `a279` / `steam:367520:235591978:chunk:001` (Hollow Knight) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Hollow Knight is a once-in-a-lifetime experience. Its deeply immersive worldbuilding and masterful storytelling come together to create an unforgettable narrative. The phenomenal soundtrack also deserves every bit of praise it receives. Christopher Larkin was truly cooking, and many tracks have found their way into my everyday playlists. The gameplay is equally impressive. Many boss fights feel like a dance as you weave in attacks and spells while dodging each strike. Having 100%ed this game and explored every corner of its world, I can’t recommend it highly enough to anyone looking for a rewarding time sink.
  - `a381` / `steam:367520:237310292:chunk:001` (Hollow Knight) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Hollow Knight is an amazing game with beautiful artstyle, phenomenal soundtrack and very very deep lore, but sometimes it can be difficult. It is probably the best purchase in my life and is worth recommending. Also GAME IS FILLED WITH BUGS.
  - `a002` / `steam:367520:236128627:chunk:001` (Hollow Knight) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Absolute Masterpiece! (9/10) ​Hollow Knight is a breathtaking journey through a beautifully dark and tragic world. Everything in this game comes together perfectly to create an unforgettable experience: ​Gameplay &amp; Combat: Silky-smooth mechanics, tight controls, and rewarding progression. ​Music &amp; Atmosphere: The soundtrack and visual design are hauntingly beautiful, drawing you deep into the lore of Hallownest. ​Challenge: The difficulty is tough and demanding, but incredibly satisfying to master. ​Final Score: 9 / 10 The only reason it’s not a full 10/10 is the lack of official Arabic lauage support. Other than that, it is near perfection. Highly recommended to anyone who loves Metroidvanias and great storytelling!
- c2 中文：一条评论特别提到 Christopher Larkin 的配乐，认为它融入了圣巢遗迹与失落王国的氛围。
  - English: One review highlights Christopher Larkin's score as part of the atmosphere of Hallownest's ruins and forgotten kingdom.
  - `a719` / `steam:367520:236368691:chunk:001` (Hollow Knight) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Hollow Knight is not just a game; it is a descent into a haunting, melancholic dream.  Team Cherry didn&#x27;t just build a world; they unearthed a vast, forgotten kingdom. Hallownest is a masterclass in atmospheric storytelling, where every rain-soaked ruin in the City of Tears and every shadow in Deepnest echoes with lost glory, backed by Christopher Larkin’s achingly gorgeous score.  The gameplay is surgical perfection. Tight, responsive controls, the hypnotic satisfaction of nail-pogoing, and boss fights that demand absolute respect and rhythm. You get lost, you adapt, you overcome.  An untouchable monument of the Metroidvania genre and an absolute work of art. 10/10.

## q03 · Cyberpunk 2077 最近在 PC 上运行得怎么样？

How well does Cyberpunk 2077 run on PC these days?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
One reviewer reports that their PC cannot run Cyberpunk 2077 even at the lowest graphics settings.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
一位评论者表示，即使使用最低画质设置，自己的 PC 也无法运行 Cyberpunk 2077。

**Review note / 核验提示**
严格参考集合只有一条直接的性能差评。不要把画面好看推断为 PC 运行流畅。
Only one direct performance complaint is in the strict reference set. Avoid inferring smooth performance from praise of graphics.

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：一位评论者表示，即使使用最低画质设置，自己的 PC 也无法运行 Cyberpunk 2077。
  - English: One reviewer reports that their PC cannot run Cyberpunk 2077 even at the lowest graphics settings.
  - `a256` / `steam:1091500:237091103:chunk:001` (Cyberpunk 2077) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: i absolutely love cyberpunk on my console, however i bought it for my pc to play it with mods only to find utter disappointment. my pc cant even run this game on the worst possible graphics even with a 4060. its not the fps part but the fact that it has latency delay like crazy. it feels like im playing on an old 70 inch tv with how bad the delay is. i cant aim properly because of it or do much of anything as it makes me motion sick. i looked it up and its an extremely common problem for this game that even downloading mods, updating and tweaking my drivers, and tons of research hasn&#x27;t fixed at all. cant even refund it because i took too much time trying to resolve the issue. 0/10 on pc.

## q04 · Monster Hunter Wilds 运行流畅吗，还是存在帧率问题？

Does Monster Hunter Wilds run smoothly, or is the frame rate a problem?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
PC performance reports vary: one reviewer gets 50–60 FPS on a low-end PC with some drops in the Plains, while another reports about 50 FPS with balanced DLSS and no frame generation. Some reviewers report smooth laptop play or improvements after fixes, whereas another still needs frame generation; performance therefore depends on the reported setup and experience.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
PC 性能体验各不相同：一位评论者在配置较低的 PC 上达到 50–60 FPS，但在平原区域仍会掉帧；另一位使用平衡模式 DLSS、不启用帧生成时约为 50 FPS。有评论者表示笔记本运行流畅，或修复后性能有所改善；另一位则仍需启用帧生成，因此体验取决于评论中报告的配置与实际情况。

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：PC 性能体验各不相同：一位评论者在配置较低的 PC 上达到 50–60 FPS，但在平原区域仍会掉帧；另一位使用平衡模式 DLSS、不启用帧生成时约为 50 FPS。
  - English: PC performance reports vary: one reviewer gets 50–60 FPS on a low-end PC with some drops in the Plains, while another reports about 50 FPS with balanced DLSS and no frame generation.
  - `a769` / `steam:2246340:237302690:chunk:001` (Monster Hunter Wilds) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: i&#x27;ve been playing for 100+ hours and i gotta say the optimization is good now since i can reach 50-60 fps even on my potato PC. but still got a slightly fps drop on Plains area tho  Focus mode in Wilds really a great help for me that has a aiming issues in MH World, wound mechanic is cool mechanic with difference effects on every weapons.  The dificulties of high star monsters is okay not really hard at first and pretty easy when you already familiar with the monster pattern and behaviour. hope they increase their dificulties in Ascendences  overall this game really addictive and i personally recomended this game to everyone who wants to play action games
  - `a775` / `steam:2246340:236769736:chunk:001` (Monster Hunter Wilds) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Probably the easiest monster hunter game to start playing if this is your first monster hunter. The story&#x27;s unskippable scenes and &quot;walks&quot; are pretty boring, and longer than I would like. In my opinion, this game has the best combat in the monster hunter series.

Combat is faster and feels way more tight compared to Monster Hunter World. Lots of nice quality of life improvements make the gameplay smoother and graphics are the best of any Monster hunter game ever. Some people seem to have performance issues but I have personally never hade a single crash or framedrop.
  - `a770` / `steam:2246340:236388593:chunk:001` (Monster Hunter Wilds) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Wilds is back baby!! It runs well on my laptop and Im running linux on it as well. Only issue I have is that i cant play with my friends because of some issues with crossplay on linux
  - `a762` / `steam:2246340:235239867:chunk:001` (Monster Hunter Wilds) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Fighting big monsters have never been more fun. Though my only gripe about MHWilds is how unstable some of the lobbies are sometimes I get dced from online play while other times it never happens just a lil shotty
  - `a134` / `steam:2246340:236655344:chunk:001` (Monster Hunter Wilds) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Wilds might be one of my top games of all time. Again I&#x27;m not a long time fan of the franchise, but from the games I have played, Wilds just feels like the most polished vision of Monster Hunter while working within the confines of the RE Engine.  Graphically, it&#x27;s beautiful, and I&#x27;m very aware that I&#x27;m fortunate enough to have experienced the game after a year of patches and QoL changes, even now I have to use frame generation to sit comfortably in the triple digit FPS range.  Despite that, I really liked the story with the Guardian monsters, and just subtle elements like bows having infinite arrow coatings was really nice as a bow main. That said, wasn&#x27;t a fan of the insect glaive&#x27;s handling of the kinsect gauges.  Very excited for Ascendance.

## q07 · 评论者如何评价 Red Dead Redemption 2 的叙事？

How do reviewers rate the narrative of Red Dead Redemption 2?

**English draft / 英文草稿**

Other supplied reviews also give strong praise to the story and characters and their contribution to the game's atmosphere.

## q09 · Hollow Knight: Silksong 的操作手感如何？

How do the controls feel in Hollow Knight: Silksong?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
Reviewers describe Silksong's movement as fluid, acrobatic, and fast, with snappy, precise combat. One reviewer says Hornet takes time to get used to, while another particularly enjoys playing with a controller.

评论者形容 Silksong 的移动流畅、灵活而迅速，战斗反应利落且精准。一位评论者表示需要时间适应 Hornet 的操作，另一位则特别喜欢使用手柄游玩。

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：评论者形容 Silksong 的移动流畅、灵活而迅速，战斗反应利落且精准。

  - `a396` / `steam:1030300:235783774:chunk:001` (Hollow Knight: Silksong) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Honestly, after waiting for what felt like forever, stepping into Pharloom as Hornet was worth every single second. I’m definitely not the most skilled gamer out there I died way more times than I’d like to admit but Hollow Knight: Silksong is an absolute masterpiece.  The movement feels so fluid and acrobatic compared to the original. Jumping, sprinting, and using Hornet’s needle craft makes combat feel like a fast-paced dance. The handcrafted art style, atmospheric music, and intricate world design continually blew me away. Every new area had me completely immersed, even when a brutal boss had me stuck for an hour.  It’s tough, but every victory feels earned and ridiculously satisfying. If you enjoy action platformers, atmospheric worlds, or just a really solid challenge, I can&#x27;t recommend this game enough. Do yourself a favor and give it a play you won&#x27;t regret it!

## q10 · Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown 的优化好吗？

Is Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown well optimized?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
The supplied reviews criticize Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown's optimization and performance; a review with 3 helpful votes reports a drop to five frames after fast travel. A review with 6 helpful votes initially reports needing minimum settings and heavy DLSS for an unstable 60 FPS at 1080p on an RTX 3070 and Ryzen 5 5600X, but its later February 2026 edit reports substantial improvement.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
这些评论批评 Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown 的优化和性能；其中一条获 3 次有用投票的评论表示，快速移动后帧率降到了 5 帧。一条获 6 次有用投票的评论最初表示，在 RTX 3070 和 Ryzen 5 5600X 上，需要最低设置并较大程度依赖 DLSS，才能在 1080p 下接近但无法稳定维持 60 FPS；不过，其 2026 年 2 月的后续更新表示性能已大幅改善。

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：这些评论批评 Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown 的优化和性能；其中一条获 3 次有用投票的评论表示，快速移动后帧率降到了 5 帧。

My game went down to 5 frames after using the fast travel option. Not to mention stretched graphical errors and frequent server maintenances.  Nacon deserves the backlash they got.  Test Drive Limited Pre-Alpha (Always Online DRM included)  People say this game has improved with the past few updates. I honestly don&#x27;t see that. Consumers needs to stop huffing that copium.  This game isn&#x27;t overhated.

## q12 · 长期粉丝如何评价 Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III 的玩法变化？

What do longtime fans think of the gameplay changes in Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
Reviews comparing Dawn of War III with earlier entries criticize its attempted blend of previous designs, MOBA-like maps, smaller battles, and the effort of managing creeps and heroes. One comparison is more favorable about base-building: it is described as improved over Dawn of War II, though less robust than the original Dawn of War.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
与前作进行比较的评论批评 Dawn of War III 混合旧设计的尝试，认为地图像 MOBA、战斗规模偏小，管理小兵和英雄也过于费力。一条比较对基地建设更为肯定：认为它比 Dawn of War II 有进步，但仍不如初代 Dawn of War 完善。

**Review note / 核验提示**
将观点归属于长期粉丝时，需要核对评论者背景；这份草稿没有采用明确表示自己是新玩家的评论。
Check reviewer background when attributing opinions to longtime fans; the explicitly new player is not used in this draft.

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：与前作进行比较的评论批评 Dawn of War III 混合旧设计的尝试，认为地图像 MOBA、战斗规模偏小，管理小兵和英雄也过于费力。
  - English: Reviews comparing Dawn of War III with earlier entries criticize its attempted blend of previous designs, MOBA-like maps, smaller battles, and the effort of managing creeps and heroes.
  - `a962` / `steam:285190:236999842:chunk:001` (Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: I will dig in my heels &amp; defend the voice acting, music &amp; art style. It&#x27;s a 5/5 on atmosphere, it is a true WH40K game. But at the end of the day, gameplay rules with an iron fist &amp; trying to merge the 2 previous games constitutions was poorly executed, if it was possible in the first place.  If you want good feels, this knocked it out of the park. But if you want a good game? only if you&#x27;re ok with overpaying...
  - `a170` / `steam:285190:214131511:chunk:001` (Warhammer 40,000: Dawn of War III) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 13: Played the campaign. Played a bunch of skirmish matches. Got wrecked in Multiplayer.  Things I like: - Animations and graphics - Unit abilities - There is some basic base building  Things I didn&#x27;t like: - No horde survival mode - Small scale battles for a 40k title - Maps feel like they were trying to make this into a MOBA - Really wanted all races, even if it was DLC  Bottom line... I reinstalled DOW Soulstorm, added the Ultimate apocalypse mod and carried on from there. Much better.

Audio and voice overs are great too. Makes you feel the urgency.  Also the power scaling of hero units, plus the tower system during skirmishes gives it a MOBA vibe. **English draft / 英文草稿**
Views on Monster Hunter Wilds' visual trade-off differ: one reviewer praises its looks but still needs frame generation, while another praises the graphics and reports no crashes or frame drops. Other reviews criticize performance, and a review with 4 helpful votes prefers Monster Hunter World's graphics and overall presentation.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：对 Monster Hunter Wilds 画面与性能的取舍，评论者看法不同：一位喜欢画面但仍需帧生成，另一位也称赞画面，并表示没有崩溃或掉帧。
  - English: Views on Monster Hunter Wilds' visual trade-off differ: one reviewer praises its looks but still needs frame generation, while another praises the graphics and reports no crashes or frame drops.
  - `a134` / `steam:2246340:236655344:chunk:001` (Monster Hunter Wilds) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 0: Wilds might be one of my top games of all time. Again I&#x27;m not a long time fan of the franchise, but from the games I have played, Wilds just feels like the most polished vision of Monster Hunter while working within the confines of the RE Engine. Other supplied reviews criticize PC optimization in Warzone, Redfall, and Red Dead Redemption 2 without giving the same kind of hardware-specific FPS comparison.

## q93 · Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown 的驾驶操作和帧率如何？

How do the driving controls and frame rate feel in Test Drive Unlimited Solar Crown?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
Opinions on Solar Crown's driving differ: some criticize handling, while others enjoy the driving; a review with 1 helpful vote finds the early cars decent, and a review with 4 helpful votes appreciates how older cars feel different. A review with 6 helpful votes initially reports unstable 60 FPS at 1080p with minimum settings and heavy DLSS on an RTX 3070 and Ryzen 5 5600X, then describes substantial improvement in a February 2026 edit. Other reviews still criticize optimization, including a report of five frames after fast travel.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
对 Solar Crown 驾驶的看法不同：有评论批评操控，也有评论喜欢驾驶；一条获 1 次有用投票的评论认为早期车辆手感尚可，另一条获 4 次有用投票的评论欣赏老车不同的驾驶感受。一条获 6 次有用投票的评论最初表示，在 RTX 3070 和 Ryzen 5 5600X 上，使用最低设置并较大程度依赖 DLSS，在 1080p 下仍无法稳定达到 60 FPS；随后在 2026 年 2 月的更新中表示性能大幅改善。其他评论仍然批评优化，包括快速移动后降至 5 帧的报告。

**Review note / 核验提示**
请保留早期车辆、2026 年 2 月更新等条件；不要把不同玩家的报告合并成同一套硬件体验。
Retain early-car and February-2026 update qualifications; different reports should not be merged into one hardware setup.

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：对 Solar Crown 驾驶的看法不同：有评论批评操控，也有评论喜欢驾驶；一条获 1 次有用投票的评论认为早期车辆手感尚可，另一条获 4 次有用投票的评论欣赏老车不同的驾驶感受。

## q94 · Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 的剧情和战斗相比如何？

How do the story and combat of Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 compare?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
Clair Obscur: Expedition 33 receives praise for emotional storytelling and distinctive combat, though a review with 3 helpful votes finds the story slows in the middle before picking up again. Its turn-based combat adds real-time parrying and dodging; some enjoy the challenge, while others find the strict timing exhausting or the parry system excessive. A review with 4 helpful votes praises the story until Act 3 but reports losing interest afterward.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**

## q95 · Dawn of War III 的多人对局和 MOBA 式玩法如何？

How are the multiplayer matches and MOBA-style gameplay in Dawn of War III?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
Dawn of War III reviewers criticize its MOBA-style design, smaller-scale battles, and the demands of managing heroes and creeps. Multiplayer experiences vary: some reviews find matches fun, challenging, or enjoyable with friends, while a review with 13 helpful votes reports its author being heavily defeated. One reviewer also praises base-building as an improvement over Dawn of War II, though less robust than the original game.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
Dawn of War III 评论者批评其 MOBA 式设计、较小的战斗规模，以及管理英雄和小兵的负担。多人体验不同：有评论认为对局有趣、有挑战，或适合与朋友游玩；另一条获 13 次有用投票的评论则表示，作者自己被打得很惨。一位还肯定基地建设相比 Dawn of War II 有进步，但仍不如初代完善。

- c1 中文：Dawn of War III 评论者批评其 MOBA 式设计、较小的战斗规模，以及管理英雄和小兵的负担。

## q96 · Call of Duty: Warzone 玩起来稳定、流畅吗？

Is Call of Duty: Warzone stable and smooth to play?

**English draft / 英文草稿**
Some Warzone reviewers report network lag, packet bursts, desynchronization, disconnects, performance problems, and updates that break things. A review with 2 helpful votes reports 65–75 FPS at maximum settings without upscaling on its author's PC, illustrating a different reported experience rather than universal smoothness.

**Chinese translation / 中文对照**
部分 Warzone 评论者报告网络延迟、数据包突发、不同步、断线、性能问题，以及更新后出现新问题。另一条获 2 次有用投票的评论表示，作者的 PC 在最高设置、不启用分辨率放大时可达到 65–75 FPS；这反映了不同的个人体验，而不是普遍流畅的结论。

**Evidence / 证据：**

- c1 中文：部分 Warzone 评论者报告网络延迟、数据包突发、不同步、断线、性能问题，以及更新后出现新问题。
  - English: Some Warzone reviewers report network lag, packet bursts, desynchronization, disconnects, performance problems, and updates that break things.
  - `a022` / `steam:1962663:227694526:chunk:001` (Call of Duty: Warzone) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 41: Warzone is an absolute joke at this point. The servers are some of the worst I&#x27;ve ever seen in a major online game. Constant lag, packet bursts, desync, random disconnects, and getting killed around corners make every match feel broken. It doesn&#x27;t matter how good your aim or positioning is when the servers decide who wins the fight.  What&#x27;s even worse is the endless plague of cheaters. Every other match seems to have someone with suspicious tracking, wallhacks, or blatant aimbotting. It&#x27;s beyond frustrating to spend 20 minutes looting and fighting just to get wiped by someone who clearly isn&#x27;t playing legitimately. The anti-cheat system feels completely ineffective.
  - `a301` / `steam:1962663:213616368:chunk:001` (Call of Duty: Warzone) · 有用投票 / helpful votes: 2: Warzone used to be fun, but now it’s just frustrating. A lot of players don’t like it because the game feels unbalanced, matchmaking is overly sweaty, and cheaters still ruin matches.
